FILE unique_cost_http.py
"""Actual lock contention for 400 distinct drug-bearing pieces within answer cap."""
from pathlib import Path
import concurrent.futures,hashlib,json,os,socket,subprocess,sys,time,urllib.request,urllib.error
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;ROOT=OUT.parents[2];PORT=8893;BASE=f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}'
env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8','TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
def post(path,payload):
 t=time.perf_counter();req=urllib.request.Request(BASE+path,json.dumps(payload).encode(),{'Content-Type':'application/json'})
 try:r=urllib.request.urlopen(req,timeout=20)
 except urllib.error.HTTPError as ex:r=ex
 with r:body=json.load(r);status=r.status
 return {'path':path,'input':payload,'status':status,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-t,'body':body}
sock=socket.socket();sock.bind(('127.0.0.1',PORT));sock.close()
before=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
with (OUT/'unique_cost_server.txt').open('w',encoding='utf8') as log:
 p=subprocess.Popen([sys.executable,'-B','-u','src/app.py',str(PORT)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
 try:
  for _ in range(100):
   if p.poll() is not None:raise RuntimeError('server exit')
   try:urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=1).read();break
   except OSError:time.sleep(.1)
  else:raise RuntimeError('server startup timeout')
  baseline=post('/api/explore',{'text':'EGFR'})
  text=';'.join(f'isa x{i:04d}' for i in range(400));assert len(text)==3999
  with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
   start=time.perf_counter();leader=pool.submit(post,'/api/check',{'text':'EGFR','answer':text});time.sleep(.1);dispatched=time.perf_counter()-start;follower=pool.submit(post,'/api/explore',{'text':'EGFR'})
   t=time.perf_counter();r=urllib.request.urlopen(BASE,timeout=3);r.read();live={'seconds':time.perf_counter()-t,'status':r.status}
   cold=leader.result();queued=follower.result()
  warm=post('/api/check',{'text':'EGFR','answer':text});after_req=post('/api/explore',{'text':'EGFR'})
  result={'baseline':baseline,'cold':cold,'queued_egfr':queued,'follower_dispatched_seconds':dispatched,'live_get':live,'warm':warm,'after':after_req}
  (OUT/'isolated_unique_cost_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf8');print(json.dumps({k:{'status':v['status'],'seconds':v['seconds']} for k,v in result.items() if isinstance(v,dict)},indent=2))
 finally:
  p.terminate();p.wait(timeout=10);after=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
  with socket.socket() as sock:closed=sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',PORT))!=0
  (OUT/'unique_cost_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid':p.pid,'port':PORT,'port_closed':closed,'db_before':before,'db_after':after,'db_unchanged':before==after,'exit':p.returncode},indent=2),encoding='utf8')


FILE cost_stress.py
"""End-to-end linker/check cost under HTTP's input and answer character caps."""
import json,statistics,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
from app import Engine
e=Engine();e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
cases=[('plain_'+str(n),' '.join(['x']*n)) for n in (250,500,1000,2000)]
cases += [('split_2000',';'.join(['x']*2000)),('many_matched',';'.join(['osimertinib']*333)),
          ('many_refused',';'.join(['Mvasi bevacizumab']*222)),
          ('many_joiners',' and '.join(['x']*666))]
rows=[]
for name,value in cases:
    times=[]
    for _ in range(3):
        start=time.perf_counter(); ents=e.kg.link_entities(value); times.append(time.perf_counter()-start)
    start=time.perf_counter(); result=e.check('EGFR',value); check=time.perf_counter()-start
    rows.append({'name':name,'text':value,'characters':len(value),'link_seconds':times,
                 'link_median_seconds':statistics.median(times),'check_seconds':check,
                 'entity_count':len(ents),'evidence_count':result['evidence_count']})
e.kg.conn.close()
(OUT/'cost_stress.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
print(json.dumps([{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('text','link_seconds')} for r in rows],indent=2))

FILE fresh_cost.py
"""Bounded, same-data cost comparison and owned CLI lock-contention check."""
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 8894
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
PREFIX = "isolated_" if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "isolated" else ""
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))


def dump(name, obj):
    (OUT / (PREFIX + name)).write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf8")


if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "worker":
    import query
    ref, kind, size = sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4])
    mod = query
    if ref != "HEAD":
        mod = types.ModuleType("cost_previous")
        mod.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/query.py")
        sys.modules[mod.__name__] = mod
        code = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{ref}:src/query.py"], cwd=ROOT).decode("utf8")
        exec(compile(code, mod.__file__, "exec"), mod.__dict__)
    kg = mod.OncoKG()
    kg.conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
    value = {"plain": " ", "split": ";", "slash": "/"}[kind].join(["x"] * size)
    times = []
    for _ in range(3):
        start = time.perf_counter()
        entities = kg.link_entities(value)
        times.append(time.perf_counter() - start)
    detail = {}
    if ref == "HEAD":
        for name in ["_resolver_blocked", "_link"]:
            start = time.perf_counter()
            result = getattr(kg, name)(value)
            detail[name] = {"seconds": time.perf_counter() - start, "result_count": len(result)}
    kg.conn.close()
    print(json.dumps({"ref": ref, "kind": kind, "size": size, "characters": len(value),
                      "times": times, "median": statistics.median(times),
                      "entities": entities, "stages": detail}))
    raise SystemExit(0)


def post(path, payload, timeout=20):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    raw = response.read()
    return {"path": path, "payload": payload, "status": response.status,
            "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start, "bytes": len(raw), "body": json.loads(raw)}

"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 8899
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
CASES = [
    "bevacizumab_awwb", "bevacizumab__awwb", "bevacizumab-awwb",
    "bevacizumab (awwb)", "bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab-(awwb)",
    "[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab", "89-Zr trastuzumab",
    "[89 Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab",
    "bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib", "bevacizumab",
    "Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate", "CHEMBL5315030",
    "Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container", "CHEMBL1355736",
    "40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin", "CHEMBL1908360",
]

def post(endpoint, text):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    with response:
        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
                "response": json.loads(response.read())}

with socket.socket() as sock:
    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
with (OUT / "independent_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
    results = []
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
            try:
                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
                break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.1)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
        for name in CASES:
            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
        (OUT / "independent_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
        (OUT / "independent_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

perf_probe.py
"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))

if len(sys.argv) > 1:
    import query
    import textrules as T
    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
    module = query
    if mode == 'm4':
        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
    kg = module.OncoKG()
    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
            'single': 'x' * size,
            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
        original = getattr(T, name)
        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
            calls[name] += 1
            calls['characters'] += len(value)
            return original(value)
        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
    kg.conn.close()
    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
else:
    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
    rows = []
    for kind, size in cases:
        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
            try:
                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
            rows.append(row)
            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

FILE attacks/FINDINGS.md
# Round 10 independent originator and tracer attacks

Reviewed `c364c84` against the supplied round-10 brief. No application or earlier audit file was edited. Both scripts run against the live snapshot through a read-only SQLite connection after Engine construction; bytecode generation is disabled. All files from this worker are under this directory.

## Reproducible evidence

- `python -B audit_evidence_claims/m10/attacks/probe.py`: **1,224 inputs**, recording raw resolver, resolver veto, list linker, whole-name shortcut, public linker, map, and full dictionary hit. [Full output](probe_results.json), [candidate positives](positive_summary.json). **396 Drug maps are a triage population, not 396 findings**: controls, chemical locants, genuinely labelled stored drugs, and explicit list-policy boundary cases are included.
- `python -B audit_evidence_claims/m10/attacks/focus.py`: **34 exact inputs** against current and exact parent-commit query modules (`3a362eb4f156ea252301d18507ff0a733d0b601e`), each with map, trial QA and sponsor QA. [Output](focus_results.json), [HTTP-ready input list](http_inputs.json). All other application code/data are held current; these are query-module counterfactuals, not a reconstructed old deployment.
- Root performs actual original-CLI HTTP confirmation independently. This worker's evidence above is direct Engine execution, not HTTP.

## Blocking candidates, exact inputs

### A. New regression: a stored name absorbs its trailing isotope before `_marked` checks adjacent words

`A33 124i` returns **ADAKITUG / CHEMBL5314551**, with antibody props and its graph map, instead of refusal. The dictionary explicitly contains `A33 124i` as a ChEMBL synonym of ADAKITUG. `T.is_isotope_label("124i")` is true (`src/textrules.py:289-298`), and exact parent query code refuses this exact input. Current `A33 124 i`, `A33 [124i]`, and `A33 124I` also draw ADAKITUG; `124i A33` refuses. The preferred-name control `ADAKITUG` remains available. ADAKITUG has **zero live trials**, so both QA forms have zero evidence; this finding does not claim invented trial evidence.

Every current stage misses the tracer distinction: raw resolver ALIAS to CHEMBL5314551; resolver veto `{}`; list linker Drug; whole-name shortcut Drug for the exact `A33 124i`; final map ADAKITUG. At `src/query.py:248-252`, `own` is `["A33", "124i"]`, but only `own[:1]` and `own[:2]` are tested. Neither finds the trailing label. The match has already consumed that label, so it is absent from `nxt`. The exact-name shortcut (`258-275`) consequently receives no refusal and returns the same wrong identity. This is the tracer-alias exception risk expressly cited by the round-9 repair note; the new adjacent-name implementation does not cover the end of an accepted name itself.

### B. New regression: the lone isotope-shaped alias allowance is applied inside longer inputs

`18F fluorothymidine` and `18-F fluorothymidine` draw **FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18 / CHEMBL2030434 / 1 live trial**, even though the supplied name is not fludeoxyglucose. Trial QA and sponsor QA each return **one fludeoxyglucose evidence row**. The stronger vocabulary-only control `18F nonexistingdrug` does the same. The dictionary has a standalone `18f` synonym for this drug; `fluorothymidine` itself is absent. Exact parent query code refused all three longer inputs; both versions accept the permitted lone `18f` control.

For the unbracketed forms, raw resolver is UNRESOLVED, resolver veto `{}`, list/public linker accepts only the `18F`/`18-F` token, whole-name shortcut is `None`, and final map is FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18. No refusal stage fires. `_marked` explicitly excludes `ws == own` (`src/query.py:252`) without requiring this token to be the entire item. Therefore an isotope token's presence in the alias index can turn a different or unknown labelled substance into that alias's therapeutic graph. `src/query.py:384-402` then records that token as a complete Drug hit. This is separate from valid lone `566C`/`9-ac`/`5ac` exceptions and from the recorded over-refusals.

### C. Inherited hole: two-token slicing happens before punctuation-only tokens are removed

`trastuzumab 89 - Zr`, `trastuzumab [89 - Zr]`, and `trastuzumab 89 _ Zr` draw **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 live trials**, with **12 trial and 12 sponsor QA rows**. Controls `trastuzumab 89 Zr`, `trastuzumab 89-Zr`, and `trastuzumab [89.Zr]` refuse, as does current prefix `89 - Zr trastuzumab`. This is inside one list item; no comma/slash/list-word policy ambiguity is involved.

Raw resolver accepts the unlabelled drug; veto is `{}`; list/public linker emits Drug without Refused; whole-name is `None`. At `src/query.py:245`, `after` is limited to two original whitespace tokens (`89`, `-`) **before** `_alnum_words` at `250` discards `-`. `Zr` is never examined. The user specified one or two **alnum words, any separator**; this code instead budgets punctuation as a word. Exact parent query code also leaks, so this is a newly reported inherited hole, not a claim that the latest commit introduced it.

### D. Inherited multiword qualifier normalization holes

`generic-version of bevacizumab` and `generic_version of bevacizumab` draw **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 live trials**, and each QA form has **12 rows**. `albumin - bound paclitaxel` draws **PACLITAXEL / CHEMBL428647 / 234 trials**, also **12/12 QA rows**. `follow-on-biologic bevacizumab` leaks likewise. Controls `generic version of bevacizumab`, `albumin-bound paclitaxel`, and `follow-on biologic bevacizumab` refuse.

All three guard stages miss; no Refused hit occurs. `_qualified` only preserves whitespace or folds punctuation to hyphen (`src/query.py:127-134`), whereas the existing patterns require whitespace between `generic`/`version` and `on`/`biologic`, or at most one whitespace-or-hyphen between `albumin`/`bound` (`src/textrules.py:142-149`). For example the underscore form becomes `generic-version`, which still fails `generic\s+version`. All are inherited in the parent-module comparison. These are clear product qualifiers, rather than isotope or alias classification guesses.

## Cases deliberately not promoted to blockers

Slash/comma/semicolon/pipe separators can detach suffix or isotope words, but these conflict with the expressly adopted list-item boundary policy, so their maps are retained as observations only. `89Zr labelled trastuzumab` and element-first notation are outside the narrow just-adjacent label design; no separate blocker is asserted for those. The known M5-S2/rapamycin/ADC over-refusals are not reclassified as blockers. Stored names whose chemical locants resemble isotopes and true isotope-bearing drugs require individual identity assessment; the broad alias-scan positives do not establish wrongdoing by themselves.

## Spec axis (under 400 words)

The brief requires that a refused product never draw its originator and asks whether the map draws anything the input does not name. Current code still violates that outcome for `A33 124i` (new suffix-isotope regression), `18F fluorothymidine` (new isotope-token substitution), `trastuzumab 89 - Zr` (inherited separation hole), and explicit product descriptions such as `albumin - bound paclitaxel` (inherited pattern hole). The current design specifically promises isotope inspection in one or two alnum words with any separator; inspecting only two raw tokens and only the beginning of a matched alias is partial implementation. Existing names and metadata controls continue to distinguish accepted bare molecules from qualified products. Evidence and counterfactuals above support changes required; no unrelated feature or scope change is requested.

290: ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS = {
291:     "f", "c", "n", "o", "ga", "cu", "zr", "i", "lu", "tc", "in", "y", "at",
292:     "ac", "pb", "re", "sm", "sr", "ra", "rb", "br", "h", "p", "s",
293: }
294: 
295: 
296: def is_isotope_label(text: str) -> bool:
297:     m = RE_ISOTOPE.match((text or "").strip())
298:     return bool(m and m.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
339: def canonical_key(text: str) -> str:
340:     """Aggressive key used for dictionary lookup (never shown to a user)."""
341:     t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
342:     t = "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c))
343:     t = t.lower()
344:     t = t.replace("’", "'").replace("‐", "-").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
345:     t = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "", t)
346:     return t

## Current boundaries and regressions

M9-S1 is split rather than given one blanket verdict: Theophylline is repaired, while both hydroxyethyl rapamycin spellings and the Trastuzumab ADC spelling remain the explicitly accepted over-refusals. M5-S2 also remains accepted. They are not newly classified as blockers. Unchanged unsupported lowercase IDs/investigator-label lookup, prefixed comma-name QA and nonparenthetical reversed-qualifier behavior remain **NOT FIXED** with their original policy qualifications.

`_qualified` now folds accents/underscores/punctuation (`src/query.py:119-138`), `_marked` applies suffix and adjacent isotope checks (`src/query.py:231-255`), the positive matcher refuses a marked hit (`src/query.py:384-395`), and `_whole_name` waits for those refusals (`src/query.py:258-276`). These changes repair M9's exact examples. The fresh attack result is broader: `_marked` does not inspect every isotope-bearing location inside a complete matched alias, so a successful old-reproduction suite cannot establish universal refusal safety. Root/attack evidence records the independently verified new blocker rather than calling M9's original examples unfixed.

The 7,511-key comparison finds 19 newly refused aliases versus m9 (18 known FDA-suffix forms and `lipo-dox`), plus 16 restored inputs. Restored isotope-bearing aliases are not all labelled defects: several identify their own isotope-bearing stored node. `A33 124i` is the actionable originator candidate passed to the attack worker. [Comparison inputs and exact identities](names_m5_attack.json).

All seven original QA demos retain evidence counts **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**; six whole responses match original bfcb83d, Mvasi differs only in refusal metadata. [Demo comparison](names_demo_results.json). UI and cost validation are separate.

```text
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m10/names/m9_attacks/probe.py
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m10/names/run_final_replays.py
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m10/names/build_findings.py
```

`run_round9.py` keeps its inherited name for provenance; it runs the relocated scripts against current HEAD. The historical baseline comparisons swap only the stated old query methods/module; app, rules and database remain current. No application code changed, so the conditional KOA/KhOpenApi original-versus-modified code requirement does not apply.

+"""Replay original cost workloads sequentially; no other audit benchmark runs alongside."""
+import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1', PYTHONUTF8='1', PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',
+           TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'), TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'), TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+rows = []
+names = ['cache_boundary_http.py'] if 'cache' in sys.argv else [
+    'run_perf_current.py', 'cost_stress.py', 'unique_piece_cost.py',
+    'unique_cost_http.py', 'fresh_cost.py']
+for name in names:
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', str(OUT/name)], cwd=ROOT, env=env,
+                       capture_output=True, timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append(dict(script=name, exit_code=p.returncode, seconds=time.perf_counter()-start))
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]), flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

Findings below use the repository's own isotope/product predicates, dictionary identities and controlled spellings; they do not require external clinical assumptions.
+
+## Blocking findings
+
+### M10-B1 — P1, new regression: a stored alias absorbs its trailing isotope
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"A33 124i"}`.
+
+**Actual:** HTTP 200, **ADAKITUG / CHEMBL5314551**, no refusal. Its map draws the stored **ACTS_ON → CXCL8 (ENSG00000169429), INHIBITOR** edge. The centre has **0 trials**; trial and sponsor QA both have zero evidence, so this is not a claim of invented trial rows. The dictionary records `A33 124i` as an alias of the unlabelled ADAKITUG antibody. The repository identifies `124i` as an isotope (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Exact parent query code refused this input. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [dictionary, stages and predecessor](attacks/focus_results.json).
+
+| Current stage | `A33 124i` result |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | ALIAS → CHEMBL5314551 |
+| Resolver veto | `{}` |
+| Item/public linker | Drug ADAKITUG, no Refused |
+| Whole-name shortcut | Drug ADAKITUG |
+| Map | ADAKITUG with its CXCL8 relation |
+
+**Cause:** `_marked` computes `own = ["A33", "124i"]`, but checks only `own[:1]` and `own[:2]`, not the trailing isotope (`src/query.py:248-252`). The accepted match already consumed `124i`, so it is no longer in the adjacent `nxt` words. The whole-name shortcut receives no veto and accepts the same stored alias (`258-275`). This recreates the stored tracer-alias risk explicitly discussed in the round-9 repair note without adding an explicit exemption.
+
+Controls/variants: `A33 124 i`, `A33 [124i]`, and `A33 124-I` also select ADAKITUG; preferred name `ADAKITUG` correctly remains available. `124i A33` is **not_found**, not a successful refusal control. Do not generalize this finding to every restored isotope-bearing stored name: several other names identify an explicitly labelled drug node themselves.
+
+**Required outcome:** an isotope at the end of an accepted stored alias must not disappear from the refusal decision merely because the positive matcher consumed it.
+
+### M10-B2 — P1, new regression: an isotope token is substituted for the named substance
+
+Exact input **`18F fluorothymidine`** returns HTTP 200, **FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18 / CHEMBL2030434 / 1 trial**, no refusal. Trial QA returns its **NCT00923273** evidence row; sponsor QA has one row. **`18F nonexistingdrug`** gives the same map and evidence, proving that recognizing the isotope token alone is sufficient to invent a positive identity for an unknown labelled substance. `18-F fluorothymidine` and `[18F] fluorothymidine` also reproduce. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/isotope_http.json), [full QA/stages](attacks/focus_results.json).
+
+For the unbracketed inputs every refusal stage misses: raw resolver **UNRESOLVED**, veto `{}`, item/public linker matches only **`18F`**, whole-name `None`. The dictionary has a standalone `18f` synonym for fludeoxyglucose; the remainder does not name that molecule. Exact parent query code refused the longer inputs. Both versions permit the lone `18f` alias control, so preserving valid lone-name behavior does not require this regression.
+
+**Cause:** `_marked` excludes any isotope test equal to the matched name itself via **`ws != own`** (`src/query.py:252`) without requiring that token to be alone in its item. The positive matcher then emits it as a Drug (`384-402`). The protection intended for lone isotope-shaped names is thus applied inside a longer product description. Bare `fluorothymidine` is not_found, but prefixing an isotope produces an unrelated positive map.
+
+**Required outcome:** keep supported lone aliases while preventing an isotope word from replacing the unknown or different substance it labels. This is a wrong selected identity, not a fabricated graph edge.
+
+### M10-B3 — P1, inherited hole: punctuation consumes the two-word isotope budget
+
+Exact inputs **`trastuzumab 89 - Zr`** and **`trastuzumab [89 - Zr]`** return HTTP 200, **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 trials**, no refusal. Trial QA returns **12** originator rows; direct sponsor QA also returns **12**. `trastuzumab 89 - Zr, osimertinib` still selects TRASTUZUMAB. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [complete stages/QA](attacks/focus_results.json).
+
+Raw resolver accepts the unlabelled drug; resolver veto is `{}`; item/public linker emits Drug without Refused; whole-name is `None`. Controls **`trastuzumab 89 Zr`**, **`trastuzumab 89-Zr`**, and **`trastuzumab [89.Zr]`** refuse. Current prefix **`89 - Zr trastuzumab`** also refuses. These are single-item inputs, with no comma/slash/list-word interpretation needed.
+
+**Cause:** `after` takes only two original whitespace tokens at **`src/query.py:245`**: `89` and `-`. `_alnum_words` removes `-` only afterward (`250`), so `Zr` never reaches the isotope predicate. The promised limit is one or two **alnum words, any separator**, not two raw tokens. The underscore-separator variant also leaks. Parent query code leaks too; this is newly reported inherited incompleteness, not a claim that M9's exact repaired examples regressed.
+
+**Required outcome:** apply the adjacency budget to the normalized alnum words rather than letting punctuation-only tokens exhaust it.
+
+### M10-B4 — P1, inherited hole: normalized multiword product qualifiers still miss every guard
+
+| Exact input | HTTP map | Trial QA evidence | Refusing control |
+|---|---|---:|---|
+| `generic-version of bevacizumab` | BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials | 12 | `generic version of bevacizumab` |
+| `albumin - bound paclitaxel` | PACLITAXEL / CHEMBL428647 / 234 trials | 12 | `albumin-bound paclitaxel` |
+
+Both are HTTP 200 without refusal. Sponsor QA also has 12 rows for each. `generic_version of bevacizumab` and `follow-on-biologic bevacizumab` are additional direct reproductions. Raw resolver, resolver veto and item linker all miss; whole-name supplies no veto. Exact parent query code leaks too. [Independent HTTP and controls](root_checks/candidate_http.json), [stages and parent comparison](attacks/focus_results.json).
+
+**Cause:** `_qualified` retains whitespace or folds punctuation to hyphen (`src/query.py:125-138`), but the underlying patterns require whitespace in `generic\s+version` and `on\s+biologic`, or at most one whitespace/hyphen in `albumin[\s\-]?bound` (`src/textrules.py:142-149`). Thus underscore folding still produces an unrecognized `generic-version`, and spaces around a hyphen defeat `albumin-bound`. The words qualify the same product within one item; this is separate from the accepted list-boundary policy.
+
+**Required outcome:** equivalent separator forms of an explicit product qualifier must preserve refusal before its originator is accepted. Bare molecule and separately listed valid-drug controls must remain distinguishable.
+

All original originator-blocking inputs are **FIXED**; continued limitations and accepted over-refusals remain individually **NOT FIXED**. M2 stopped without a REPORT; its partial role/order and name fixtures are included through the completed M2b/cumulative replays. Repeated rows reflect repeated qualifications rather than independent defects.

"""Verify original tracked bytes and review artifacts; never mutate the application."""
from pathlib import Path
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import re
import socket
import subprocess

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
before = json.loads((OUT / 'root_checks/integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

def git(*args):
    got = subprocess.run(['git', *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {'code': got.returncode, 'stdout': got.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace'),
            'stderr': got.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}

changed = []
missing = []
for rel, digest in before['files'].items():
    file = ROOT / rel
    if not file.exists():
        missing.append(rel)
    elif hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
        changed.append(rel)
tracked_now = set(git('ls-files', '-z')['stdout'].split('\0')) - {''}
ports = {}
for port in [8891, 8892, 8893, 8894, 8895, 8897, 8898, 8899, 8901]:
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        sock.settimeout(.2)
        ports[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
links = re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', report)
broken = [s for s in links if s != 'integrity_final.json' and not (OUT / s).exists()]
db_hash = hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
result = {
    'time': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
    'head_before': before['head'], 'head_after': git('rev-parse', 'HEAD')['stdout'].strip(),
    'tracked_files_hashed': len(before['files']), 'changed_tracked_files': changed,
    'missing_tracked_files': missing, 'new_tracked_paths': sorted(tracked_now-set(before['files'])),
    'tracked_diff': git('diff', '--exit-code'), 'staged_diff': git('diff', '--cached', '--exit-code'),
    'git_status': git('status', '--short'),
    'database_sha256_before': before['db_sha256'], 'database_sha256_after': db_hash,
    'database_unchanged': before['db_sha256'] == db_hash,
    'owned_ports_closed': ports, 'report_links_checked': len(links),
    'broken_report_links': broken,
    'report_final_line': report.rstrip().splitlines()[-1],
    'report_sha256': hashlib.sha256((OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
}
result['passed'] = (not changed and not missing and not result['new_tracked_paths']
    and result['head_before'] == result['head_after']
    and result['tracked_diff']['code'] == result['staged_diff']['code'] == 0
    and result['database_unchanged'] and all(ports.values()) and not broken
    and result['report_final_line'] == 'VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED')
(OUT / 'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
assert result['passed'], 'Final integrity verification failed; inspect JSON'
